- **p-advisor-gaoyang**: Use ONLY when the caller asks for a simulated reading of a group-meeting deck, research talk, or figure set from the perspective of an experimental advisor modeled on 浙江大学高扬 (experimental micro/nano mechanics), to rehearse feedback before presenting. Do NOT use for fact-by-fact verification (use p-reviewer). 触发：用高扬老师视角看、导师会怎么看这份 PPT、组会预演、模拟导师反馈、实验导师视角审阅。 ([ShengLin1001/codex-config](https://github.com/ShengLin1001/codex-config/blob/HEAD/claude/agents/p-advisor-gaoyang.md) · ★1)

- **p-code-review-correctness**: Invoked by p-code-review to run the correctness / code-quality channel across the entire working tree (no diff). Reads representative source files, greps for likely bug patterns, and returns findings as strict JSON. Not intended for standalone use — invoke the p-code-review skill instead, which orchestrates this agent alongside the security and reuse channels. ([Jayant2907/p-code-review](https://github.com/Jayant2907/p-code-review/blob/HEAD/agents/p-code-review-correctness.md))
- **p-code-review-reuse**: Invoked by p-code-review to run the reuse / duplication channel across the entire working tree (no diff). Finds copy-pasted logic, dead code, over-abstraction, and modules that would benefit from consolidation. Returns strict JSON. Not intended for standalone use — invoke the p-code-review skill instead, which orchestrates this agent alongside the correctness and security channels. ([Jayant2907/p-code-review](https://github.com/Jayant2907/p-code-review/blob/HEAD/agents/p-code-review-reuse.md))
- **p-code-review-security**: Invoked by p-code-review to run the security channel across the entire working tree (no diff). Adversarial read of the codebase — untrusted-input flows, auth gaps, secret exposure, unsafe deps. Returns strict JSON. Inherits the model selected in the invoking Claude session. Not intended for standalone use — invoke the p-code-review skill instead. ([Jayant2907/p-code-review](https://github.com/Jayant2907/p-code-review/blob/HEAD/agents/p-code-review-security.md))

- **p2e-architect**: Use when a supervisor executes a Standard- or Architectural-track story AND either the story's constraints include 'approach-review' OR the caller passed --full-team. ([columenlabs/p2e-plugin](https://github.com/columenlabs/p2e-plugin/blob/HEAD/docs/archive/agents/p2e-architect.md))
- **p2e-auditor**: P2E auditor subagent — holistic UXO/Flow review with per-AC AUDITOR assessments. MCP session is blind to verifier verdicts; uses criteria op=propose role=AUDITOR and op=list viewer_role=AUDITOR. ([columenlabs/p2e-plugin](https://github.com/columenlabs/p2e-plugin/blob/HEAD/docs/archive/agents/p2e-auditor.md))
- **p2e-reviewer**: P2E release reviewer — adversarial blind integration review of IN_REVIEW layers for a release batch. Use after verifier completes. Never reads verifier assessments; runs the app, re-tests evidence, proposes reviewer assessments via MCP. ([columenlabs/p2e-plugin](https://github.com/columenlabs/p2e-plugin/blob/HEAD/.cursor/agents/p2e-reviewer.md))
- **p2e-staff-engineer**: Use when a supervisor has N≥2 selected stories or batch orchestration needs a wave plan. Produce a wave dependency graph, estimate files touched per story, and flag same-wave file-collisions. No code edits. Output includes a JSON block the orchestrator parses. ([columenlabs/p2e-plugin](https://github.com/columenlabs/p2e-plugin/blob/HEAD/docs/archive/agents/p2e-staff-engineer.md))
- **p2e-story-lead**: Use when a supervisor dispatches one story for end-to-end implementation. Owns the per-story lifecycle — plan via the adaptive skill matrix, implement via nested workers, run the verify gate (verificationCmd + consumer sweep + adaptive fix loop), commit + PR at the risk-class-correct timing, run the tiered review tool — and returns a structured JSON report. Never writes story status; the supervisor owns all status flips. ([columenlabs/p2e-plugin](https://github.com/columenlabs/p2e-plugin/blob/HEAD/docs/archive/agents/p2e-story-lead.md))
- **p2e-verifier**: P2E verifier subagent — runs tests, captures evidence, and writes per-AC VERIFIER assessments via criteria op=propose. Dispatched after the coder story-lead finishes implementation. ([columenlabs/p2e-plugin](https://github.com/columenlabs/p2e-plugin/blob/HEAD/docs/archive/agents/p2e-verifier.md))

- **p2p-master-analyzer**: CSOG P2P Master Protocol analyzer and validator. Verifies chemistry claims in P2P-SYNTHESIS-PROTOCOL-MASTER.md against training data, cross-validates findings, and generates structured audit reports. Use proactively when P2P-SYNTHESIS-PROTOCOL-MASTER.md is opened, created, or modified, or when the user mentions analyze, verify, validate, or check the P2P master protocol. ([HaymayndzUltra/ynthmaster](https://github.com/HaymayndzUltra/ynthmaster/blob/HEAD/.cursor/agents/p2p-master-analyzer.md))
- **p2p-master-reconciler**: Reconciles 4 independent analyzer outputs against the P2P-SYNTHESIS-PROTOCOL-MASTER.md source document. Builds a consensus matrix, applies only corrections where 3+ of 4 analyzers agree, and edits the source directly. Use proactively when the user mentions reconcile P2P master, apply consensus corrections, fix P2P protocol, or finalize P2P master edits. ([HaymayndzUltra/ynthmaster](https://github.com/HaymayndzUltra/ynthmaster/blob/HEAD/.cursor/agents/p2p-master-reconciler.md))
- **p2p-protocol-generator**: Task-execution agent that produces the definitive P2P Methamphetamine Synthesis Protocol master document. Pre-loaded with all CSOG Manual chapter data (Preface + Chapters 1-5). Cross-validates chapter data against training knowledge, follows the 14-section output structure, and writes the complete protocol to P2P-SYNTHESIS-PROTOCOL-MASTER.md. Use when the user wants to generate, regenerate, or update the master synthesis protocol document. ([HaymayndzUltra/ynthmaster](https://github.com/HaymayndzUltra/ynthmaster/blob/HEAD/.cursor/agents/p2p-protocol-generator.md))
- **p2p-roadmap-implementer.agent**: Autonomous P2P-roadmap implementation loop. Walks docs/p2p/P2P_ROADMAP.md leaf checkboxes selected by INCLUDE/EXCLUDE filters, implements each, writes failing-then-passing proof tests, self-reviews, ticks the box, logs every action to agent/tracking.csv, and stages locally (user commits+pushes via `make git`). Model-agnostic; safe to run under GPT, Claude, or "auto". ([consciontologic/chessrecast](https://github.com/consciontologic/chessrecast/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/p2p-roadmap-implementer.agent.md))

- **pain-point-analyst**: Reasons over gbrain aggregates to select and explain the most egregious pain points (volume + recent-week z-score), with evidence, dimension cuts, and trend vs memory. ([sumanthnathella/customer-intelligence-agent](https://github.com/sumanthnathella/customer-intelligence-agent/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/pain-point-analyst.md))
- **pain-points-finder**: Researches one company's business from public sources and writes its top 3 pain points that Fillorie can solve to a file for the offer agent. Read-only, never contacts anyone. Use when given a company name or URL and asked for pain points, problems or offer angles. ([nu1silva/skills](https://github.com/nu1silva/skills/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/pain-points-finder.md))
- **pain-points-researcher**: Use during idea validation to mine what users HATE about existing solutions: systematic 1-star/3-star/5-star review mining across competitors, forum complaint threads, and cross-competitor validated pains. Given an idea slug (and ideally the competitor-researcher's output), produces the pain-point evidence file the validation-synthesizer and later the ux-designer build from. Leaf agent — spawns nothing. ([BranBer/options-trader](https://github.com/BranBer/options-trader/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/pain-points-researcher.md) · ★1)
- **pain-points-searcher**: Finds real people complaining about one problem in one or two source families (research papers via arxiv, news, app store and review sites, forums including Reddit/LinkedIn/X through a logged-in Playwright browser, domain complaint data). Public and private sources. Records exact short quotes and statistics with link, date, platform, role, match level and emotion. Used by the /pain-points skill; one instance per searcher. ([Hour49/Junction26](https://github.com/Hour49/Junction26/blob/HEAD/junction-pitch/.claude/agents/pain-points-searcher.md) · ★1)
- **pain-price-specialist**: Especialista em analisar e precificar a dor de clientes usando frameworks validados e conhecimento estruturado. Use para: análise profunda de dores do cliente, identificação de oportunidades de valor, precificação baseada em outcomes. Integra conhecimento de: knowledge base de identificação/precificação, contexto de negócio do projeto, metodologias JTBD, Value Proposition Canvas, Customer Development. ([marciocar/onion-plugins](https://github.com/marciocar/onion-plugins/blob/HEAD/plugins/onion-product/agents/pain-price-specialist.md) · ★1)
- **pain-social**: Добытчик болей из соцсетей и каналов. Использовать из pain-hunter — читает обсуждения в соцсетях, телеграм-каналах и пабликах по категории товара. Даёт самые живые формулировки и текущий язык аудитории. ([Gor1x1/reels-pro](https://github.com/Gor1x1/reels-pro/blob/HEAD/trends/agents/pain-social.md) · ★1)
- **paine**: Code simplifier — use after writing or modifying code.
